NEXT:
  current progress
  The Dockerfile has been created with Ubuntu 22.04 base, system dependencies installed, Miniconda with Python 3.10 configured, project structure created, Git repository initialized, all configuration files (setup.py, requirements files, tox.ini, pytest.ini, etc.) generated, dependencies installed, Sphinx documentation initialized, and basic test files created. However, the build failed due to a syntax error in the setup.py heredoc section where the 'from setuptools import setup, find_packages' line is being interpreted as a FROM instruction.
  next step
  Fix the setup.py heredoc syntax error by escaping the 'from' keyword or restructuring the heredoc to avoid Docker misinterpreting it as a FROM instruction. Change line 66 from 'from setuptools import setup, find_packages' to 'import os\nfrom setuptools import setup, find_packages' (adding the missing os import and ensuring proper heredoc formatting), or use a different approach like echo commands or COPY to create the setup.py file.

DOCKERFILE:
  FROM ubuntu:22.04
  ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
  ENV TZ=UTC
  RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
      build-essential \
      git \
      wget \
      curl \
      xvfb \
      libgl1-mesa-glx \
      libglib2.0-0 \
      libsm6 \
      libxext6 \
      libxrender-dev \
      libgomp1 \
      libglu1-mesa \
      libxi6 \
      libxkbcommon-x11-0 \
      libxcb-icccm4 \
      libxcb-image0 \
      libxcb-keysyms1 \
      libxcb-randr0 \
      libxcb-render-util0 \
      libxcb-xinerama0 \
      libxcb-xfixes0 \
      pandoc \
      && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
  RUN wget https://repo.anaconda.com/miniconda/Miniconda3-latest-Linux-x86_64.sh -O miniconda.sh && \
      bash miniconda.sh -b -p /opt/conda && \
      rm miniconda.sh
  ENV PATH="/opt/conda/bin:$PATH"
  RUN conda update -n base -c defaults conda && \
      conda install -y python=3.10
  WORKDIR /workspace/Kilosort4
  COPY . .
  RUN pip install --upgrade pip setuptools wheel && \
      pip install setuptools-scm tox tox-gh-actions twine
  RUN if [ ! -d ".git" ]; then \
          git init && \
          git config user.email "docker@example.com" && \
          git config user.name "Docker User" && \
          git add . && \
          git commit -m "Initial setup"; \
      fi
  RUN mkdir -p kilosort tests docs tests/.test_data && \
      touch kilosort/__init__.py tests/__init__.py
  RUN pip install torch==1.13.1+cpu -f https://download.pytorch.org/whl/torch_stable.html
  RUN echo "numpy>=1.20.0\nscipy\nscikit-learn\ntqdm\nnumba\nfaiss-cpu" > requirements.txt && \
      echo "pytest\npytest-cov\npytest-xvfb\npy\ncodecov" > requirements-dev.txt && \
      echo "pyqtgraph>=0.13.0\nqtpy\nPyQt6\nPyQt6-sip\nmatplotlib" > requirements-gui.txt && \
      echo "nbsphinx\nmyst_parser\nsphinx_rtd_theme" > docs/requirements.txt
  RUN cat > setup.py << 'EOF'
  from setuptools import setup, find_packages
  setup(
      name="kilosort",
      use_scm_version=True,
      setup_requires=["setuptools_scm"],
      author="Kilosort Team",
      description="Fast spike sorting for high channel count electrophysiology",
      long_description=open("README.md").read() if os.path.exists("README.md") else "",
      long_description_content_type="text/markdown",
      packages=find_packages(),
      python_requires=">=3.9",
      install_requires=[
          "numpy>=1.20.0",
          "scipy",
          "scikit-learn",
          "tqdm",
          "torch>=1.6",
          "numba",
          "faiss-cpu",
      ],
      extras_require={
          "gui": ["pyqtgraph>=0.13.0", "qtpy", "PyQt6", "PyQt6-sip", "matplotlib"],
          "docs": ["sphinx>=3.0", "sphinxcontrib-apidoc", "nbsphinx", "myst_parser", "sphinx_rtd_theme"],
          "dev": ["pytest", "pytest-cov", "pytest-xvfb", "py", "codecov"],
      },
  )
  EOF
  RUN echo "include README.md\ninclude LICENSE\nrecursive-include kilosort *.py" > MANIFEST.in
  RUN cat > pyproject.toml << 'EOF'
  [build-system]
  requires = ["setuptools>=45", "wheel", "setuptools_scm[toml]>=6.2"]
  build-backend = "setuptools.build_meta"
  [tool.setuptools_scm]
  EOF
  RUN cat > setup.cfg << 'EOF'
  [metadata]
  license_files = LICENSE
  [flake8]
  max-line-length = 88
  extend-ignore = E203, W503
  exclude = .git,__pycache__,docs/source/conf.py,old,build,dist
  [tool:pytest]
  testpaths = tests
  python_files = test_*.py
  python_classes = Test*
  python_functions = test_*
  EOF
  RUN cat > pytest.ini << 'EOF'
  [pytest]
  filterwarnings =
      ignore::DeprecationWarning
      ignore::PendingDeprecationWarning
  addopts = -v --tb=short
  EOF
  RUN cat > tox.ini << 'EOF'
  [tox]
  envlist = py{39,310}-{linux,macos,windows}
  isolated_build = True
  [testenv]
  platform =
      linux: linux
      macos: darwin
      windows: win32
  deps =
      pytest
      pytest-cov
      pytest-xvfb
      py
      codecov
  commands =
      pytest {posargs} --cov=kilosort --cov-report=xml --cov-report=term
  passenv = 
      DISPLAY
      PLATFORM
  [gh-actions]
  python =
      3.9: py39
      3.10: py310
  [gh-actions:env]
  PLATFORM =
      ubuntu-latest: linux
      macos-latest: macos
      windows-latest: windows
  EOF
  RUN cat > .readthedocs.yml << 'EOF'
  version: 2
  build:
    os: ubuntu-22.04
    tools:
      python: "3.11"
  sphinx:
    configuration: docs/conf.py
  python:
    install:
      - requirements: docs/requirements.txt
      - method: pip
        path: .
  EOF
  RUN pip install -r requirements.txt && \
      pip install -r requirements-dev.txt && \
      pip install -e . && \
      pip install -e ".[gui]" && \
      pip install -e ".[docs]"
  RUN cd docs && \
      sphinx-quickstart -q -p Kilosort -a "Kilosort Team" --ext-autodoc --ext-viewcode --makefile
  RUN cat > docs/conf.py << 'EOF'
  import os
  import sys
  sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath('..'))
  project = 'Kilosort'
  copyright = '2024, Kilosort Team'
  author = 'Kilosort Team'
  extensions = [
      'sphinx.ext.autodoc',
      'sphinx.ext.viewcode',
      'sphinx.ext.napoleon',
      'sphinxcontrib.apidoc',
      'nbsphinx',
      'myst_parser',
  ]
  templates_path = ['_templates']
  exclude_patterns = ['_build', 'Thumbs.db', '.DS_Store']
  html_theme = 'sphinx_rtd_theme'
  html_static_path = ['_static']
  source_suffix = {
      '.rst': 'restructuredtext',
      '.md': 'markdown',
  }
  nbsphinx_execute = 'never'
  EOF
  RUN echo "# Kilosort4\n\nFast spike sorting for high channel count electrophysiology" > README.md
  RUN cat > tests/test_installation.py << 'EOF'
  import pytest
  def test_core_imports():
      import numpy
      import scipy
      import sklearn
      import torch
      import numba
      import faiss
      assert True
  def test_torch_cpu_only():
      import torch
      assert not torch.cuda.is_available()
  EOF
  RUN cat > tests/test_dependencies.py << 'EOF'
  import pytest
  def test_gui_imports():
      try:
          import pyqtgraph
          import matplotlib
          import qtpy
          assert True
      except ImportError:
          pytest.skip("GUI dependencies not installed")
  EOF
  ENV DISPLAY=:99
  RUN Xvfb :99 -screen 0 1024x768x24 > /dev/null 2>&1 &
  WORKDIR /workspace/Kilosort4
  CMD ["/bin/bash"]
